// Ù¾Ø§Ù„Øª Ø±Ù†Ú¯ÛŒ ÙˆØ§Ù‚Ø¹â€ŒÚ¯Ø±Ø§ÛŒØ§Ù†Ù‡ Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Û¶ Ø±Ù†Ú¯ Ú¯Ù„Ø¯Ø§Ù† (Ø¨Ù‡ Ù‡Ù…Ø±Ø§Ù‡ Ø³Ø§ÛŒÙ‡ Ùˆ Ø®Ø·ÙˆØ· Ø¨Ø±Ø¬Ø³ØªÙ‡)
const getPotPalette = (potColor: PotColorType | string) => {
  switch (potColor) {
    case 'black':
      return { fill: '#262B30', shade: '#1B1F23', stroke: '#111417', detail: '#3B424A' };
    case 'mint':
      return { fill: '#CBEAD9', shade: '#B2DEC7', stroke: '#7BAF96', detail: '#94C9AE' };
    case 'terracotta':
      return { fill: '#D97B54', shade: '#C26540', stroke: '#8F4326', detail: '#E69573' };
    case 'grey':
      return { fill: '#9CA3AF', shade: '#848C99', stroke: '#555E6B', detail: '#B6BDC7' };
    case 'cream':
      return { fill: '#F5EFE0', shade: '#EAE0C8', stroke: '#C8BCA2', detail: '#DDD2B8' };
    case 'white':
    default:
      return { fill: '#FFFFFF', shade: '#F2EFE9', stroke: '#CFC8B8', detail: '#E2DDD0' };
  }
};

// ØªØ´Ø®ÛŒØµ Ù‡ÙˆØ´Ù…Ù†Ø¯ Ù…Ø¯Ù„ Ú¯Ù„Ø¯Ø§Ù† Ø¯Ø± ØµÙˆØ±ØªÛŒ Ú©Ù‡ Ø¨Ù‡ Ú©Ø§Ù…Ù¾ÙˆÙ†Ù†Øª Ù¾Ø§Ø³ Ø¯Ø§Ø¯Ù‡ Ù†Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø¨Ø§Ø´Ø¯
const resolveEffectivePotShape = (shape: string, explicitPotShape?: PotShapeType): PotShapeType => {
  if (explicitPotShape) return explicitPotShape;
  if (shape === 'combo_trough' || shape === 'combo_trough_2') return 'trough_fluted';
  if (shape === 'christmas_cactus' || shape === 'climbing_rose' || shape === 'rose_bush') {
    return 'chalice_urn';
  }
  if (shape === 'sansevieria') return 'square_patterned';
  if (shape === 'aglaonema' || shape === 'zz' || shape === 'sedum' || shape === 'dieffenbachia') {
    return 'faceted_gem';
  }
  if (shape === 'dracaena' || shape === 'tradescantia') return 'cylinder_modern';
  return 'round_ribbed';
};

            <ellipse cx="39" cy="21" rx="2.2" ry="1.2" fill="#D4C7B8" />
            <ellipse cx="61" cy="22" rx="2.2" ry="1.2" fill="#D4C7B8" />

        const seedStr = `${shape}_${plantName}`;
        let hash = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < seedStr.length; i++) {
          hash = (hash * 31 + seedStr.charCodeAt(i)) % 10000;
        }
        const leafCount = 5 + (hash % 3);
        const palettes = [
          ['#1B4332', '#2D6A4F', '#52B788', '#95D5B2'],
          ['#14532D', '#166534', '#22C55E', '#BBF7D0'],
          ['#365314', '#4D7C0F', '#84CC16', '#ECFCCB'],
          ['#134E4A', '#0F766E', '#14B8A6', '#99F6E4']
        ];
        const chosenColors = palettes[hash % palettes.length];
        const hasFlowers =
          plantName.includes('Ú¯Ù„') ||
          plantName.includes('Ø¨Ù†ÙØ´Ù‡') ||
          plantName.includes('Ø´Ù…Ø¹Ø¯Ø§Ù†ÛŒ') ||
          plantName.includes('Ø§Ø±Ú©ÛŒØ¯Ù‡');

        return (
          <g>
            <path d="M50 54V24M50 42L36 30M50 38L64 28" stroke={chosenColors[0]} strokeWidth="2.3" strokeLinecap="round" />
            {Array.from({ length: leafCount }).map((_, idx) => {
              const spread = (idx - (leafCount - 1) / 2) * 18;
              const cx = 50 + spread * 0.85;
              const cy = 24 + Math.abs(spread) * 0.45;
              return (
                <g key={idx} transform={`rotate(${spread} ${cx} ${cy})`}>
                  <ellipse
                    cx={cx}
                    cy={cy}
                    rx="7.5"
                    ry="13.5"
                    fill={chosenColors[(idx % 2) + 1]}
                    stroke={chosenColors[0]}
                    strokeWidth="1.2"
                  />
                  <line x1={cx} y1={cy - 9} x2={cx} y2={cy + 10} stroke={chosenColors[3]} strokeWidth="1" opacity="0.75" />
                </g>
              );
            })}
            {hasFlowers && (
              <g>
                <circle cx="43" cy="18" r="3.6" fill="#EC4899" stroke="#9D174D" strokeWidth="0.9" />
                <circle cx="57" cy="19" r="3.4" fill="#F43F5E" stroke="#9D174D" strokeWidth="0.9" />
                <circle cx="50" cy="14" r="4" fill="#E11D48" stroke="#881337" strokeWidth="0.9" />
              </g>
            )}
          </g>
        );
      }
    }
  };

  return (
    <svg className="w-23 h-23 drop-shadow-xs" viewBox="0 0 100 90" fill="none">
      {renderFoliage()}
      {renderPotGeometry()}
    </svg>
  );
};

interface AddPlantModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  gardens: GardenEntity[];
  nextIndex: number;
  onClose: () => void;
  onAdd: (newPlant: PlantEntity) => void;
}

export const AddPlantModal: React.FC<AddPlantModalProps> = ({

  nextIndex,
  onClose,
  onAdd

export type MemberRole = 'super_admin' | 'space_admin' | 'caretaker' | 'viewer';

export interface MemberPermissions {
  canWaterAndMist: boolean;
  canEditPlants: boolean;
  canManageSpaces: boolean;
  canDelete: boolean;
}

export interface MemberEntity {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  role: MemberRole;
  assignedSpaces: string[];
  assignedGardens?: string[];
  pinCode?: string;
  permissions?: MemberPermissions;
  telegramChatId: string;
  telegramUsername?: string;
  notifyAlerts: boolean;
  notifyActions: boolean;
  active: boolean;
}

export const getDefaultMemberPermissions = (role: MemberRole): MemberPermissions => {
  switch (role) {
    case 'super_admin':
    case 'space_admin':
      return {
        canWaterAndMist: true,
        canEditPlants: true,
        canManageSpaces: true,
        canDelete: true
      };
    case 'caretaker':
      return {
        canWaterAndMist: true,
        canEditPlants: false,
        canManageSpaces: false,
        canDelete: false
      };
    case 'viewer':
    default:
      return {
        canWaterAndMist: false,
        canEditPlants: false,
        canManageSpaces: false,
        canDelete: false
      };
  }
};

  name: string;

  family: string;

  customSvgMarkup?: string;
}

const GEMINI_KEY_STORAGE = 'sina_greenhouse_gemini_key_v1';

export const getGeminiApiKey = (): string => {
  const local = localStorage.getItem(GEMINI_KEY_STORAGE);
  if (local && local.trim().length > 0) return local.trim();
  const envKey = import.meta.env.VITE_GEMINI_API_KEY as string | undefined;
  return envKey ? envKey.trim() : '';
};

export const saveGeminiApiKey = (key: string): void => {
  localStorage.setItem(GEMINI_KEY_STORAGE, key.trim());
};

const CONFIG_STORAGE_KEY = 'sina_greenhouse_cloud_config_v1';
const LAST_UPDATE_ID_KEY = 'sina_greenhouse_tg_offset_v4';

// ØªÙˆØ§Ø¨Ø¹ Ú©Ù…Ú©ÛŒ Ø§ÛŒÙ…Ù† Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ø§Ù†ØªØ®Ø§Ø¨ Ø§ÙˆÙ„ÛŒÙ† Ø±Ø´ØªÙ‡ Ù¾Ø± Ùˆ ØªØ±Ú©ÛŒØ¨ Ø´Ø±Ø·â€ŒÙ‡Ø§
const pickStr = (...values: Array<string | undefined | null>): string => {
  for (const val of values) {
    if (typeof val === 'string' && val.trim().length > 0) {
      return val.trim();
    }
  }
  return '';
};

const toPersianNum = (num: number | string): string =>
  String(num).replace(/\d/g, (d) => 'Û°Û±Û²Û³Û´ÛµÛ¶Û·Û¸Û¹'[Number(d)]);

export const copyTextSafe = async (text: string): Promise<boolean> => {
  try {
    if (navigator.clipboard && window.isSecureContext) {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      return true;
    }
  } catch {
    // Ø§Ø¯Ø§Ù…Ù‡ Ø¨Ù‡ Ø±ÙˆØ´ Ø¬Ø§ÛŒÚ¯Ø²ÛŒÙ†
  }

  try {
    const textArea = document.createElement('textarea');
    textArea.value = text;
    textArea.style.position = 'fixed';
    textArea.style.left = '-9999px';
    textArea.style.top = '0';
    document.body.appendChild(textArea);
    textArea.focus();
    textArea.select();
    const copied = document.execCommand('copy');
    document.body.removeChild(textArea);
    return copied;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
};

export const getCloudConfig = (): CloudConfig => {
  const saved = localStorage.getItem(CONFIG_STORAGE_KEY);
  const envSupabaseUrl = import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL as string | undefined;
  const envSupabaseKey = import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY as string | undefined;
  const envBotToken = import.meta.env.VITE_TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN as string | undefined;
  const envChatId = import.meta.env.VITE_TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID as string | undefined;
  const envBotUsername = import.meta.env.VITE_TELEGRAM_BOT_USERNAME as string | undefined;

  if (saved) {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(saved);
      return {
        supabaseUrl: pickStr(parsed.supabaseUrl, envSupabaseUrl),
        supabaseAnonKey: pickStr(parsed.supabaseAnonKey, envSupabaseKey),
        telegramBotToken: pickStr(parsed.telegramBotToken, envBotToken),
        telegramChatId: pickStr(parsed.telegramChatId, envChatId),
        telegramBotUsername: pickStr(parsed.telegramBotUsername, envBotUsername),
        browserPushEnabled: Boolean(parsed.browserPushEnabled)
      };
    } catch {
      // Ø¨Ø§Ø²Ú¯Ø´Øª Ø¨Ù‡ Ù…Ù‚Ø§Ø¯ÛŒØ± Ù¾ÛŒØ´â€ŒÙØ±Ø¶
    }
  }

  return {
    supabaseUrl: pickStr(envSupabaseUrl),
    supabaseAnonKey: pickStr(envSupabaseKey),
    telegramBotToken: pickStr(envBotToken),
    telegramChatId: pickStr(envChatId),
    telegramBotUsername: pickStr(envBotUsername),
    browserPushEnabled: false
  };
};

export const saveCloudConfig = (config: CloudConfig): void => {
  localStorage.setItem(CONFIG_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(config));
};

let supabaseInstance: SupabaseClient | null = null;

export const getSupabaseClient = (): SupabaseClient | null => {
  const cfg = getCloudConfig();
  if (!cfg.supabaseUrl) return null;
  if (!cfg.supabaseAnonKey) return null;
  if (!supabaseInstance) {
    supabaseInstance = createClient(cfg.supabaseUrl, cfg.supabaseAnonKey);
  }
  return supabaseInstance;
};

export const fetchGreenhouseFromCloud = async (): Promise<GreenhouseCloudPayload | null> => {
  const client = getSupabaseClient();
  if (!client) return null;

  const { data, error } = await client
    .from('greenhouse_state')
    .select('payload')
    .eq('room_id', ROOM_ID)
    .single();

  if (error) return null;
  if (!data?.payload) return null;

  if (data.payload.botSettings) {
    const currentCfg = getCloudConfig();
    const remoteBot = data.payload.botSettings;
    saveCloudConfig({
      ...currentCfg,
      telegramBotToken: pickStr(currentCfg.telegramBotToken, remoteBot.telegramBotToken),
      telegramChatId: pickStr(currentCfg.telegramChatId, remoteBot.telegramChatId),
      telegramBotUsername: pickStr(currentCfg.telegramBotUsername, remoteBot.telegramBotUsername)
    });
  }

  return data.payload as GreenhouseCloudPayload;
};

export const pushGreenhouseToCloud = async (payload: {
  spaces: SpaceEntity[];
  plants: PlantEntity[];
  gardens: GardenEntity[];
  members: MemberEntity[];
}): Promise<boolean> => {
  const client = getSupabaseClient();
  if (!client) return false;

  const cfg = getCloudConfig();
  const fullPayload = {
    ...payload,
    botSettings: {
      telegramBotToken: cfg.telegramBotToken,
      telegramChatId: cfg.telegramChatId,
      telegramBotUsername: cfg.telegramBotUsername
    }
  };

  const { error } = await client.from('greenhouse_state').upsert(
    {
      room_id: ROOM_ID,
      payload: fullPayload,
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
    },
    { onConflict: 'room_id' }
  );

  return !error;
};

export const fetchTelegramBotUsername = async (
  customToken?: string
): Promise<string | null> => {
  const cfg = getCloudConfig();
  const fallbackUsername = pickStr(cfg.telegramBotUsername);
  const token = pickStr(customToken, cfg.telegramBotToken);
  if (!token) {
    return fallbackUsername ? fallbackUsername : null;
  }

  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 3500);

  try {
    const res = await fetch(`https://greenhouse-bot.sinafallah-ai.workers.dev/bot${token}/getMe`, {
      signal: controller.signal
    });
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    if (!res.ok) {
      return fallbackUsername ? fallbackUsername : null;
    }
    const data = await res.json();
    const username: string = pickStr(data?.result?.username);
    if (username) {
      saveCloudConfig({ ...cfg, telegramBotUsername: username });
      return username;
    }
    return fallbackUsername ? fallbackUsername : null;
  } catch {
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    return fallbackUsername ? fallbackUsername : null;
  }
};

export const setupTelegramBotCommands = async (customToken?: string): Promise<boolean> => {
  const cfg = getCloudConfig();
  const token = pickStr(customToken, cfg.telegramBotToken);
  if (!token) return false;

const cachedSubscribersMap = new Map<string, TelegramBotSubscriber>();

export const fetchRecentBotSubscribers = async (
  customToken?: string
): Promise<TelegramBotSubscriber[]> => {
  const cfg = getCloudConfig();
  const token = pickStr(customToken, cfg.telegramBotToken);
  if (!token) return Array.from(cachedSubscribersMap.values());

  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 4000);

  try {
    const res = await fetch(`https://greenhouse-bot.sinafallah-ai.workers.dev/bot${token}/getUpdates?limit=30`, {
      signal: controller.signal
    });
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    if (!res.ok) return Array.from(cachedSubscribersMap.values());
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data?.ok && Array.isArray(data.result)) {
      for (const item of data.result) {
        const msg = item.message
          ? item.message
          : item.edited_message
          ? item.edited_message
          : item.my_chat_member;
        const chat = msg?.chat;
        const text: string = pickStr(msg?.text);
        if (chat && chat.id) {
          const cid = String(chat.id);
          let invitedMemberId: string | undefined = undefined;
          if (text.startsWith('/start join_')) {
            invitedMemberId = text.replace('/start join_', '').trim();
          }
          const prev = cachedSubscribersMap.get(cid);
          const fullName = [chat.first_name, chat.last_name].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
          const finalName = pickStr(fullName, chat.title, 'Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø± ØªÙ„Ú¯Ø±Ø§Ù…');

          cachedSubscribersMap.set(cid, {
            chatId: cid,
            firstName: finalName,
            username: chat.username,
            lastText: pickStr(text, '/start'),
            invitedMemberId: finalInvite
          });
        }
      }
    }
    return Array.from(cachedSubscribersMap.values());
  } catch {
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    return Array.from(cachedSubscribersMap.values());
  }
};

const resolveTargetChatIds = (
  spaceId: string,
  members: MemberEntity[],
  mode: 'alerts' | 'actions'
): string[] => {
  const cfg = getCloudConfig();
  const targets = new Set<string>();

  members.forEach((m) => {
    if (!m.active) return;
    const chatId = pickStr(m.telegramChatId);
    if (!chatId) return;

    const isSuper = m.role === 'super_admin';
    const isAssigned = m.assignedSpaces.includes(spaceId);
    const hasSpaceAccess = isSuper ? true : isAssigned;
    if (!hasSpaceAccess) return;

    if (mode === 'alerts' && m.notifyAlerts) {
      targets.add(chatId);
    }
    if (mode === 'actions' && m.notifyActions) {
      targets.add(chatId);
    }
  });

  const fallbackChatId = pickStr(cfg.telegramChatId);
  if (targets.size === 0 && fallbackChatId) {
    targets.add(fallbackChatId);
  }

  return Array.from(targets);
};

export const sendTelegramToTargets = async (
  title: string,
  bodyHtml: string,
  targetChatIds?: string[],
  inlineKeyboard?: TelegramInlineButton[][],
  useMainReplyKeyboard: boolean = false
): Promise<TelegramSendResult> => {
  const cfg = getCloudConfig();

  if (
    cfg.browserPushEnabled &&
    'Notification' in window &&
    Notification.permission === 'granted'
  ) {
    const plainBody = bodyHtml.replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '');
    new Notification(title, { body: plainBody });
  }

  const token = pickStr(cfg.telegramBotToken);
  if (!token) {
    return { ok: false, sentCount: 0, errorMsg: 'ØªÙˆÚ©Ù† Ø±Ø¨Ø§Øª ØªÙ„Ú¯Ø±Ø§Ù… ÙˆØ§Ø±Ø¯ Ù†Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø§Ø³Øª.' };
  }

  const chatIds =
    targetChatIds && targetChatIds.length > 0
      ? targetChatIds
      : fallbackChatId
      ? [fallbackChatId]
      : [];

  if (chatIds.length === 0) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      sentCount: 0,
      errorMsg: 'Ù‡ÛŒÚ† Ú¯ÛŒØ±Ù†Ø¯Ù‡ ÙØ¹Ø§Ù„ÛŒ (Chat ID) Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ø§ÛŒÙ† Ú¯Ù„Ø®Ø§Ù†Ù‡ ØªÙ†Ø¸ÛŒÙ… Ù†Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø§Ø³Øª.'
    };
  }

  let replyMarkup: Record<string, unknown> | undefined = undefined;
  if (inlineKeyboard && inlineKeyboard.length > 0) {
    replyMarkup = { inline_keyboard: inlineKeyboard };
  } else if (useMainReplyKeyboard) {
    replyMarkup = TELEGRAM_MAIN_REPLY_KEYBOARD;
  }

  let sentCount = 0;
  for (const chatId of chatIds) {
    try {
      const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {
        chat_id: chatId,
        text: htmlMessage,
        parse_mode: 'HTML',
        disable_web_page_preview: true
      };
      if (replyMarkup) {
        payload.reply_markup = replyMarkup;
      }

      const res = await fetch(`https://greenhouse-bot.sinafallah-ai.workers.dev/bot${token}/sendMessage`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(payload)
      });
      if (res.ok) sentCount++;
    } catch {
      // Ø§Ø¯Ø§Ù…Ù‡ Ø¨Ù‡ Ú¯ÛŒØ±Ù†Ø¯Ù‡ Ø¨Ø¹Ø¯ÛŒ
    }

    errorMsg: sentCount === 0 ? 'Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ø¨Ù‡ ØªÙ„Ú¯Ø±Ø§Ù… Ù†Ø§Ù…ÙˆÙÙ‚ Ø¨ÙˆØ¯.' : undefined

export const sendInstantNotification = async (
  title: string,
  body: string
): Promise<boolean> => {
  const res = await sendTelegramToTargets(title, body);
  return res.ok;
};

const answerCallbackQuery = async (
  token: string,
  callbackQueryId: string,
  text: string
): Promise<void> => {
  try {
    await fetch(`https://greenhouse-bot.sinafallah-ai.workers.dev/bot${token}/answerCallbackQuery`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        callback_query_id: callbackQueryId,
        text,
        show_alert: false
      })
    });
  } catch {
    // Ù†Ø§Ø¯ÛŒØ¯Ù‡ Ú¯Ø±ÙØªÙ† Ø®Ø·Ø§ÛŒ Ø´Ø¨Ú©Ù‡
  }
};

const findMemberByChatId = (
  chatId: string,
  members: MemberEntity[],
  defaultChatId: string
): MemberEntity | null => {
  const found = members.find((m) => m.active && pickStr(m.telegramChatId) === chatId);
  if (found) return found;
  if (defaultChatId && chatId === defaultChatId && members.length > 0) {
    return members[0];
  }
  return null;
};

export const requestBrowserPushPermission = async (): Promise<boolean> => {
  if (!('Notification' in window)) return false;
  const permission = await Notification.requestPermission();
  return permission === 'granted';
};

export interface VersionManifest {
  version: string;
  schemaVersion: number;
  releaseDate: string;
  changelog: string[];
  forceReload?: boolean;
}

let deferredInstallPrompt: BeforeInstallPromptEvent | null = null;
const installListeners = new Set<(canInstall: boolean) => void>();

// Ø«Ø¨Øª Ø´Ù†ÙˆÙ†Ø¯Ù‡ Ø±ÙˆÛŒØ¯Ø§Ø¯ Ù†ØµØ¨ PWA Ø±ÙˆÛŒ Ù…ÙˆØ¨Ø§ÛŒÙ„ (Ø§Ù†Ø¯Ø±ÙˆÛŒØ¯/iOS) Ùˆ Ø¯Ø³Ú©ØªØ§Ù¾ (ÙˆÛŒÙ†Ø¯ÙˆØ²/Ù…Ú©)
export function initPwaInstallListener(): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;

  window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', (e: Event) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    deferredInstallPrompt = e as BeforeInstallPromptEvent;
    installListeners.forEach((cb) => cb(true));
  });

  window.addEventListener('appinstalled', () => {
    deferredInstallPrompt = null;
    installListeners.forEach((cb) => cb(false));
  });
}

export function subscribeToInstallAvailability(
  callback: (canInstall: boolean) => void
): () => void {
  installListeners.add(callback);
  callback(deferredInstallPrompt !== null);
  return () => {
    installListeners.delete(callback);
  };
}

export async function triggerPwaInstall(): Promise<'accepted' | 'dismissed' | 'unavailable'> {
  if (!deferredInstallPrompt) {
    return 'unavailable';
  }
  await deferredInstallPrompt.prompt();
  const choice = await deferredInstallPrompt.userChoice;
  if (choice.outcome === 'accepted') {
    deferredInstallPrompt = null;
    installListeners.forEach((cb) => cb(false));
  }
  return choice.outcome;
}

export function isRunningStandalonePwa(): boolean {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return false;
  const isStandalone = window.matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)').matches;
  const isIosStandalone = (window.navigator as Navigator & { standalone?: boolean }).standalone === true;
  return isStandalone || isIosStandalone;
}

// ============================================================================
// Ù…Ø¯ÛŒØ±ÛŒØª Ù†ÙˆØªÛŒÙÛŒÚ©ÛŒØ´Ù†â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ÛŒ Ø¨ÙˆÙ…ÛŒ Ø³ÛŒØ³ØªÙ…â€ŒØ¹Ø§Ù…Ù„ (Ù…ÙˆØ¨Ø§ÛŒÙ„ Ùˆ Ù„Ù¾â€ŒØªØ§Ù¾)
// ============================================================================

export function getNotificationPermissionStatus(): NotificationPermission | 'unsupported' {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !('Notification' in window)) {
    return 'unsupported';
  }
  return Notification.permission;
}

export async function requestNativeNotificationPermission(): Promise<
  NotificationPermission | 'unsupported'
> {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !('Notification' in window)) {
    return 'unsupported';
  }
  if (Notification.permission === 'granted') {
    return 'granted';
  }
  const permission = await Notification.requestPermission();
  return permission;
}

export async function sendNativeNotification(params: {
  title: string;
  body: string;
  tag?: string;
}): Promise<boolean> {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !('Notification' in window)) {
    return false;
  }
  if (Notification.permission !== 'granted') {
    return false;
  }

  try {
    if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
      const registration = await navigator.serviceWorker.getRegistration();
      if (registration) {
        await registration.showNotification(params.title, {
          body: params.body,
          tag: params.tag || 'botanica-care-alert',
          dir: 'rtl',
          lang: 'fa-IR',
          icon: '/botanica-icon.svg',
          badge: '/botanica-icon.svg'
        });
        return true;
      }
    }

    new Notification(params.title, {
      body: params.body,
      tag: params.tag || 'botanica-care-alert',
      dir: 'rtl',
      lang: 'fa-IR'
    });
    return true;
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Native notification dispatch error:', err);
    return false;
  }
}

// ============================================================================
// Ø³ÛŒØ³ØªÙ… Ø¨Ø±Ø±Ø³ÛŒ Ø¢Ù¾Ø¯ÛŒØª Ø®ÙˆØ¯Ú©Ø§Ø± (OTA Update) Ù¾Ø³ Ø§Ø² Ø§Ù†ØªØ´Ø§Ø± Ø¨Ø±Ù†Ø§Ù…Ù‡
// ============================================================================

export async function checkForRemoteAppUpdate(): Promise<VersionManifest | null> {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`/version.json?t=${Date.now()}`, {
      cache: 'no-store'
    });
    if (!res.ok) return null;
    const manifest = (await res.json()) as VersionManifest;
    if (manifest.version && manifest.version !== APP_VERSION) {
      return manifest;
    }
    return null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export async function applyAppUpdateAndReload(): Promise<void> {
  if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
    const registrations = await navigator.serviceWorker.getRegistrations();
    for (const reg of registrations) {
      await reg.update();
      if (reg.waiting) {
        reg.waiting.postMessage({ type: 'SKIP_WAITING' });
      }
    }
  }
  if ('caches' in window) {
    const keys = await caches.keys();
    await Promise.all(keys.map((key) => caches.delete(key)));
  }
  window.location.reload();
}
